VPCMPESTRM (XMM, XMM, I8) - Latency

Operands

  • Operand 1 (r): Register (XMM0, XMM1, XMM2, XMM3, XMM4, XMM5, XMM6, XMM7, XMM8, XMM9, XMM10, XMM11, XMM12, XMM13, XMM14, XMM15)
  • Operand 2 (r): Register (XMM0, XMM1, XMM2, XMM3, XMM4, XMM5, XMM6, XMM7, XMM8, XMM9, XMM10, XMM11, XMM12, XMM13, XMM14, XMM15)
  • Operand 3 (r): 8-bit immediate
  • Operand 4 (r, suppressed): Register (EAX)
  • Operand 5 (r, suppressed): Register (EDX)
  • Operand 6 (w, suppressed): Register (XMM0)
  • Operand 7 (w, suppressed): Flags (AF: w, CF: w, OF: w, PF: w, SF: w, ZF: w)

Latency operand 1 → 6: 11

Latency operand 1 → 7: ≤12

Latency operand 2 → 6: 11

Latency operand 2 → 7: ≤12

Latency operand 4 → 6: ≤16

Latency operand 4 → 7: 16

Latency operand 5 → 6: ≤16

Latency operand 5 → 7: 16

Latency operand 1 → 6: 11

Experiment 1

  • Instruction: VPCMPESTRM XMM1, XMM2, 2
  • Chain instructions: VSHUFPD XMM1, XMM0, XMM0, 0;VSHUFPD XMM1, XMM1, XMM1, 0;VSHUFPD XMM1, XMM1, XMM1, 0;VSHUFPD XMM1, XMM1, XMM1, 0;VSHUFPD XMM1, XMM1, XMM1, 0;VSHUFPD XMM1, XMM1, XMM1, 0;VSHUFPD XMM1, XMM1, XMM1, 0;VSHUFPD XMM1, XMM1, XMM1, 0;VSHUFPD XMM1, XMM1, XMM1, 0
  • Chain latency: 9
  • Code: 
       0:	c4 e3 79 60 ca 02    	vpcmpestrm xmm1,xmm2,0x2
   6:	c5 f9 c6 c8 00       	vshufpd xmm1,xmm0,xmm0,0x0
   b:	c5 f1 c6 c9 00       	vshufpd xmm1,xmm1,xmm1,0x0
  10:	c5 f1 c6 c9 00       	vshufpd xmm1,xmm1,xmm1,0x0

  2e:	c5 f1 c6 c9 00       	vshufpd xmm1,xmm1,xmm1,0x0
  • Init:
